int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);
    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &mpiRank);
    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &mpiSize);
    MPI_Comm localComm;
    MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, mpiRank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &localComm);
    int localRank;
    MPI_Comm_rank(localComm, &localRank);
    MPI_Comm_free(&localComm);
    int gpuCount = 0;
    checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&gpuCount), "discover CUDA devices");
    if (gpuCount == 0) {
        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: no CUDA device available\n", mpiRank);
        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
    }
    checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(localRank % gpuCount), "select CUDA device");
    int targetTriangles = 320;
    int timesteps = 50;
    int sourceIdx = 0;
    val_t reflectivity = 0.8f;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;

    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            targetTriangles = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-t") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            timesteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            sourceIdx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            reflectivity = static_cast<val_t>(atof(argv[++i]));
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-o") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            if (mpiRank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);
            MPI_Finalize();
            return 0;
        } else {
            if (mpiRank == 0) {
                printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
                printUsage(argv[0]);
            }
            MPI_Finalize();
            return 1;
        }
    }

    int subdivisions = getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(targetTriangles);

    if (mpiRank == 0) {
    printf("Room Response Simulation Benchmark\n");
    printf("===================================\n");
    printf("Target triangles: %d (using %d subdivisions)\n", targetTriangles, subdivisions);
    printf("Timesteps: %d\n", timesteps);
    printf("Source triangle: %d\n", sourceIdx);
    printf("Reflectivity: %.2f\n", reflectivity);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    printf("\n");
    }

    // Initialize
    SimulationState state;
    initializeSimulation(state, subdivisions, static_cast<size_t>(timesteps),
                         static_cast<size_t>(sourceIdx), reflectivity);

    if (mpiRank == 0) printf("\n");

    // Precomputation
    auto startPre = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

    computeTimeDelays(state);
    computeFormFactors(state);
    prepareDevice(state);
    if (validate) {
        std::vector<int> counts, offsets;
        rowLayout(state.numTriangles, counts, offsets, state.numTriangles);
        std::vector<val_t> globalKij;
        if (mpiRank == 0) globalKij.resize(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles);
        MPI_Gatherv(state.kij.data(), counts[mpiRank], MPI_FLOAT,
                    mpiRank == 0 ? globalKij.data() : nullptr,
                    counts.data(), offsets.data(), MPI_FLOAT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
        if (mpiRank == 0) state.kij.swap(globalKij);
    }

    auto endPre = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto preDuration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(endPre - startPre).count();

    if (mpiRank == 0) printf("Precomputation time: %ld ms\n", preDuration);
    if (mpiRank == 0) printf("\n");

    // Simulation
    auto startSim = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

    runSimulation(state);

    auto endSim = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto simDuration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(endSim - startSim).count();

    if (mpiRank == 0) { printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", simDuration); printf("\n"); }

    // Distance computation
    auto startDist = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

    computeDistances(state);

    auto endDist = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto distDuration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(endDist - startDist).count();

    if (mpiRank == 0) { printf("Distance computation time: %ld ms\n", distDuration); printf("\n"); }

    releaseDevice();

    if (mpiRank == 0) {

    // Total time
    long totalTime = preDuration + simDuration + distDuration;
    printf("Total computation time: %ld ms\n", totalTime);

    // Performance metrics
    size_t n = state.numTriangles;
    size_t t = state.numTimesteps;
    double kijOps = static_cast<double>(n * n);
    double simOps = static_cast<double>(n * n * t);
    double distOps = static_cast<double>(n * t * t);

    printf("\nPerformance:\n");
    printf("  Triangles: %zu\n", n);
    printf("  Timesteps: %zu\n", t);
    printf("  Form factor computations: %.2e\n", kijOps);
    printf("  Simulation operations: %.2e\n", simOps);
    printf("  Distance computations: %.2e\n", distOps);
    printf("  Total time per triangle: %.4f ms\n", static_cast<double>(totalTime) / n);

    // Memory usage
    size_t memKij = n * n * sizeof(val_t);
    size_t memTau = n * n * sizeof(int);
    size_t memRad = 2 * t * n * sizeof(val_t);
    size_t totalMem = memKij + memTau + memRad;
    printf("  Memory usage: %.2f MB\n", totalMem / (1024.0 * 1024.0));

    // Hash
    uint64_t hash = computeHash(state);
    printf("  Result hash: %016lX\n", hash);
    printf("\n");
    
    // Print results for external validation

+int mpiRank = 0, mpiSize = 1;

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s: %s\n", mpiRank, operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+    }
+}
+
+size_t rowBegin(size_t n, int rank) { return n * static_cast<size_t>(rank) / mpiSize; }
+size_t rowEnd(size_t n, int rank) { return n * static_cast<size_t>(rank + 1) / mpiSize; }
+
+void rowLayout(size_t n, std::vector<int>& counts, std::vector<int>& offsets, size_t stride = 1) {
+    counts.resize(mpiSize);
+    offsets.resize(mpiSize);
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < mpiSize; ++rank) {
+        size_t begin = rowBegin(n, rank) * stride;
+        size_t count = (rowEnd(n, rank) - rowBegin(n, rank)) * stride;
+        if (begin > INT_MAX || count > INT_MAX) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "MPI message exceeds INT_MAX elements\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        }
+        offsets[rank] = static_cast<int>(begin);
+        counts[rank] = static_cast<int>(count);
+    }
+}

+int mpiRank = 0, mpiSize = 1;

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s: %s\n", mpiRank, operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+    }
+}
+
+size_t rowBegin(size_t n, int rank) { return n * static_cast<size_t>(rank) / mpiSize; }
+size_t rowEnd(size_t n, int rank) { return n * static_cast<size_t>(rank + 1) / mpiSize; }
+
+void rowLayout(size_t n, std::vector<int>& counts, std::vector<int>& offsets, size_t stride = 1) {
+    counts.resize(mpiSize);
+    offsets.resize(mpiSize);
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < mpiSize; ++rank) {
+        size_t begin = rowBegin(n, rank) * stride;
+        size_t count = (rowEnd(n, rank) - rowBegin(n, rank)) * stride;
+        if (begin > INT_MAX || count > INT_MAX) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "MPI message exceeds INT_MAX elements\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        }
+        offsets[rank] = static_cast<int>(begin);
+        counts[rank] = static_cast<int>(count);
+    }
+}

+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau, const val_t* areas,
+                                const val_t* history, val_t* output, int n, int begin,
+                                int rows, int t, int source, int timeOff, val_t rho) {
+    int local = blockIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rows) return;
+    int i = begin + local;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    for (int j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[static_cast<size_t>(local) * n + j];
+        if (t < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[static_cast<size_t>(local) * n + j];
+        if (factor <= ZERO) continue;
+        val_t sourceRad = history[static_cast<size_t>(t - delay) * n + j];
+        if (sourceRad <= ZERO) continue;
+        sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * sourceRad;

+        if (threadIdx.x < step) partial[threadIdx.x] += partial[threadIdx.x + step];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0)
+        output[local] = rho * partial[0] + ((i == source && t < timeOff) ? ONE : ZERO);

+int mpiRank = 0, mpiSize = 1;

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s: %s\n", mpiRank, operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+    }
+}
+
+size_t rowBegin(size_t n, int rank) { return n * static_cast<size_t>(rank) / mpiSize; }
+size_t rowEnd(size_t n, int rank) { return n * static_cast<size_t>(rank + 1) / mpiSize; }
+
+void rowLayout(size_t n, std::vector<int>& counts, std::vector<int>& offsets, size_t stride = 1) {
+    counts.resize(mpiSize);
+    offsets.resize(mpiSize);
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < mpiSize; ++rank) {
+        size_t begin = rowBegin(n, rank) * stride;
+        size_t count = (rowEnd(n, rank) - rowBegin(n, rank)) * stride;
+        if (begin > INT_MAX || count > INT_MAX) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "MPI message exceeds INT_MAX elements\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        }
+        offsets[rank] = static_cast<int>(begin);
+        counts[rank] = static_cast<int>(count);
+    }
+}

+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau, const val_t* areas,
+                                const val_t* history, val_t* output, int n, int begin,
+                                int rows, int t, int source, int timeOff, val_t rho) {
+    int local = blockIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rows) return;
+    int i = begin + local;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    for (int j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[static_cast<size_t>(local) * n + j];
+        if (t < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[static_cast<size_t>(local) * n + j];
+        if (factor <= ZERO) continue;
+        val_t sourceRad = history[static_cast<size_t>(t - delay) * n + j];
+        if (sourceRad <= ZERO) continue;
+        sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * sourceRad;
+    }
+    __shared__ val_t partial[256];
+    partial[threadIdx.x] = sum;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (int step = blockDim.x / 2; step > 0; step >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < step) partial[threadIdx.x] += partial[threadIdx.x + step];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0)
+        output[local] = rho * partial[0] + ((i == source && t < timeOff) ? ONE : ZERO);
+}

codex
Implemented the hybrid benchmark in [roomsim.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260929-135931/roomsim_gpt-6-sol-medium_hybrid_r3/roomsim/roomsim.cpp) and updated [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260929-135931/roomsim_gpt-6-sol-medium_hybrid_r3/roomsim/CMakeLists.txt). MPI distributes receiver rows, OpenMP computes form factors and delays, and CUDA runs propagation and distance correlation. The executable remains `roomsim`.

The build succeeds. Validation passes, and result hashes match the original for the tested 20, 80, and 320 triangle cases across one to four ranks. Sampling now uses a deterministic random stream per receiver row, so individual form factors are not guaranteed to be bitwise identical to the original serial run.

+int mpiRank = 0, mpiSize = 1;

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s: %s\n", mpiRank, operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+    }
+}
+
+size_t rowBegin(size_t n, int rank) { return n * static_cast<size_t>(rank) / mpiSize; }
+size_t rowEnd(size_t n, int rank) { return n * static_cast<size_t>(rank + 1) / mpiSize; }
+
+void rowLayout(size_t n, std::vector<int>& counts, std::vector<int>& offsets, size_t stride = 1) {
+    counts.resize(mpiSize);
+    offsets.resize(mpiSize);
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < mpiSize; ++rank) {
+        size_t begin = rowBegin(n, rank) * stride;
+        size_t count = (rowEnd(n, rank) - rowBegin(n, rank)) * stride;
+        if (begin > INT_MAX || count > INT_MAX) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "MPI message exceeds INT_MAX elements\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        }
+        offsets[rank] = static_cast<int>(begin);
+        counts[rank] = static_cast<int>(count);
+    }
+}

+__global__ void propagateKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau, const val_t* areas,
+                                const val_t* history, val_t* output, int n, int begin,
+                                int rows, int t, int source, int timeOff, val_t rho) {
+    int local = blockIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rows) return;
+    int i = begin + local;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    for (int j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        if (i == j) continue;
+        int delay = tau[static_cast<size_t>(local) * n + j];
+        if (t < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[static_cast<size_t>(local) * n + j];
+        if (factor <= ZERO) continue;
+        val_t sourceRad = history[static_cast<size_t>(t - delay) * n + j];
+        if (sourceRad <= ZERO) continue;
+        sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * sourceRad;
+    }
+    __shared__ val_t partial[256];
+    partial[threadIdx.x] = sum;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (int step = blockDim.x / 2; step > 0; step >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < step) partial[threadIdx.x] += partial[threadIdx.x + step];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0)
+        output[local] = rho * partial[0] + ((i == source && t < timeOff) ? ONE : ZERO);
+}

+    int local = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (local >= rows) return;

+    val_t maxCorr = ZERO;

+    for (int t = 0; t < timesteps; ++t) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (int tt = t; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += history[static_cast<size_t>(tt - t) * n + source] *

+        if (sum > maxCorr) { maxCorr = sum; bestT = t; }

Implemented the hybrid benchmark in [roomsim.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260929-135931/roomsim_gpt-6-sol-medium_hybrid_r3/roomsim/roomsim.cpp) and updated [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260929-135931/roomsim_gpt-6-sol-medium_hybrid_r3/roomsim/CMakeLists.txt). MPI distributes receiver rows, OpenMP computes form factors and delays, and CUDA runs propagation and distance correlation. The executable remains `roomsim`.

The build succeeds. Validation passes, and result hashes match the original for the tested 20, 80, and 320 triangle cases across one to four ranks. Sampling now uses a deterministic random stream per receiver row, so individual form factors are not guaranteed to be bitwise identical to the original serial run.
